This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user tasked me with a 4-step analysis of exp_029a (SciERC 3-epoch training experiment):
   - **Step 1**: SSH to autodl-5090, check if training completed by examining logs and checkpoint directory
   - **Step 2**: If training succeeded, run inference (N=8, T=1.0, logprobs) on SciERC test set using existing project scripts, then run analysis computing degeneracy, LP delta, QE signals, within-instance rho
   - **Step 3**: Collect all metrics matching exp_028 analysis format (degeneracy CF1%, greedy/oracle F1, headroom, LP selection, 5 QE signals with AUROC/rho/selection F1, within-instance LP-F1 correlation, LP range stats)
   - **Step 4**: Update registry with status=DONE, conclusion (success if degeneracy < 17.8%, negative otherwise), and all metrics
   
   The hypothesis: SciERC 3-epoch training should produce lower degeneracy than the 5-epoch baseline (17.8%), testing if the epoch-degeneracy relationship from Few-NERD (exp_028) holds cross-dataset.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - LoRA fine-tuning (rank=32, alpha=64) of Qwen3-8B on SciERC UIE data
   - LLaMA Factory for training and LoRA export/merge
   - vLLM-based sampling inference (N=8, T=1.0 with logprobs)
   - Degeneracy measurement: Constant F1 gold-filtered rate
   - 5 Quality Estimation signals: LP (log-probability), SJ (Soft Jaccard), FK (Fleiss Kappa surface Jaccard), EM (Exact Match), VC (Voting Confidence)
   - Within-instance Spearman correlation between LP and F1
   - LP selection: best-of-N by logprob as quality estimator
   - Structured self-consistency for information extraction

3. Files and Code Sections:
   
   - **train_config_scierc_3ep.yaml** (read, existing)
     - Training config for the 3-epoch SciERC experiment
     - Key params: model=Qwen3-8B, lora_rank=32, lora_alpha=64, lr=2e-4, cosine scheduler, warmup_ratio=0.1, batch_size=2, grad_accum=8, save_steps=100, save_total_limit=3, seed=42, no load_best_model_at_end
   
   - **export_config_scierc_3epoch.yaml** (created on server)
     - LoRA merge config for the 3-epoch checkpoint
     ```yaml
     model_name_or_path: /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/Qwen/Qwen3-8B
     adapter_name_or_path: /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-scierc-3epoch-lora
     template: qwen3
     finetuning_type: lora
     export_dir: /root/autodl-tmp/struct_self_consist_ie/checkpoints/qwen3-8b-scierc-3epoch-merged
     export_size: 5
     export_device: cpu
     export_legacy_format: false
     ```
   
   - **scripts/exp_029a_postprocess/01_merge_lora.sh** (created on server)
     - Calls `llamafactory-cli export export_config_scierc_3epoch.yaml`
   
   - **scripts/exp_029a_postprocess/02_inference.sh** (created on server)
     - Runs `python code/run_mvp_pilot.py` with args: --model_path checkpoints/qwen3-8b-scierc-3epoch-merged --data_dir data/scierc/processed_data --dataset scierc --subtask ner --n_samples 8 --temperature 1.0 --seed 42 --collect_logprobs --num_test 9999 --output_dir output/exp_029a_scierc_3epoch --tensor_parallel 1
     - Uses CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0
   
   - **scripts/exp_029a_postprocess/03_analyze.py** (created on server, ~250 lines)
     - Full signal analysis adapted from exp_028's 03_analyze.py
     - INPUT_PATH: output/exp_029a_scierc_3epoch/samples.jsonl
     - BASELINE_REF: degeneracy_cf1_pct=17.8, lp_sel_delta_pp=0.42, within_instance_median_rho=0.6667
     - Computes: (1) Constant F1 degeneracy, (2) LP selection F1, (3) All 5 QE signals with AUROC/rho/selection F1, (4) Within-instance LP-F1 rho, (5) LP range stats, (6) Comparison table vs baseline, (7) Conclusion judgment
     - Success criterion: degeneracy < 17.8%
     - Imports from code/consistency.py (_extract_surface_keys, _ner_soft_jaccard_pair) and code/evaluation.py (per_instance_f1)
     - Uses sklearn.metrics.roc_auc_score for AUROC computation
     - Saves JSON results to output/exp_029a_scierc_3epoch/analysis_results.json
   
   - **scripts/exp_029a_postprocess/run_all.sh** (created on server)
     - Master script chaining: 01_merge_lora.sh → 02_inference.sh → 03_analyze.py
   
   - **code/run_mvp_pilot.py** (read, existing)
     - Main inference script supporting scierc, ace05, conll2003, wnut17, fewnerd datasets
     - Uses VLLMSampler from code/sampling.py
     - Supports --collect_logprobs flag
   
   - **scripts/exp_028_postprocess/** (read, existing template)
     - 01_merge_lora.sh, 02_inference.sh, 03_analyze.py, run_all.sh
     - Used as reference template for creating exp_029a pipeline

4. Errors and fixes:
   - ssh_read_only blocked pipe character (`|`) when trying `ps aux | grep`: Not a real error, just a tool limitation. Did not need the GPU process check since training log was being actively updated.
